SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexCoordinateBudget.real_component_le_l2
  (z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField) (i : Fin 8) :
  |(z i).re| ≤ SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2 z
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexCoordinateBudget.imag_component_le_l2
  (z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField) (i : Fin 8) :
  |(z i).im| ≤ SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2 z
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexFiniteBudget.box_l2_bound {B : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeBox.StateBox}
  {z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField} {d : ℚ}
  (hz : B.Contains (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack z)) (hd : 0 ≤ d)
  (hb : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexFiniteBudget.squaredBudget B ≤ d ^ 2) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2 z ≤ ↑d
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexFiniteBudget.tests_of_budgets {τ ε : ℝ} {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  {κ : ℝ} {previous z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField} {d update A B : ℝ} (hτ : 0 ≤ τ) (hε : 0 ≤ ε)
  (hd : 0 ≤ d) (hz : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexNormSq z = 1)
  (hdistance :
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2
        (z - -SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeCandidate b κ z) ≤
      d)
  (hupdate : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2 (z - previous) ≤ update)
  (hstep : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate τ b κ (1 / 2) previous = z)
  (hpotential :
    ∀ (i : Fin 8),
      A ≤ SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexSourcePotential b κ z i ∧
        SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexSourcePotential b κ z i ≤ B)
  (hphase : 2 * d ^ 2 ≤ 1) (hguard : τ < 1 - 2 * d ^ 2)
  (hsmall : update ≤ ε ∧ 4 * d ≤ ε ∧ (2 + (B - A)) * (d + d ^ 2) ≤ ε) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundSourceDiagnostics.MeetsConvergence τ ε b κ (1 / 2) previous z
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexFiniteBudget.fails_of_update {τ ε : ℝ} {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  {κ m : ℝ} {previous z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField}
  (hstep : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate τ b κ m previous = z)
  (hgap : ε < SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundComplexEuclidean.l2 (z - previous)) :
  ¬SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundSourceDiagnostics.MeetsConvergence τ ε b κ m previous z

  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.Tests i ↔ SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.verdict i = true
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.failed_offsets (i : Fin 18) :
  ¬SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.Tests i ↔ i = 1 ∨ i = 2 ∨ i = 17
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.passing_count :
  {i | SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.verdict i = true}.card = 15
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.failing_count :
  {i | SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSixty.verdict i = false}.card = 3
